- **hipaa-fundamentals**: Determines whether and how HIPAA applies to a product, company, or data flow, and explains the core rules with exact regulatory citations — PHI and the 18 identifiers, covered entities vs business associates, Privacy/Security/Breach Notification Rules, patient rights, and penalties.
